$(document).ready(function () {
    $(window).scroll(function () {
        var scroll = $(window).scrollTop();
        if (scroll > 60) {
            $(".main-nav").css("background", "linear-gradient(45deg, #02CCBB, #A951EE)");
            $(".main-nav").css("box-shadow", "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.42) 0px 5px 0px -12px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12) 0px 3px 20px 0px, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2) 0px 8px 5px -5px");
            $(".main-nav").css("margin-top", "0px");
        }
        else {
            $(".main-nav").css("background", "transparent");
            $(".main-nav").css("box-shadow", "none");
            $(".main-nav").css("margin-top", "30px");
        }
    })
})

$(document).on("click", ".viewmore", function () {
    if ($(this).find('i').hasClass('show-more')) {
        $(this).html('View Less Majors <i class="show-less" style="transform: scale(0.8);vertical-align: middle;"></i>');
    } else {
        $(this).html('View More Majors <i class="show-more" style="transform: scale(0.8);vertical-align: middle;"></i>');
    }
});

$(document).on("click", ".degree-view", function () {
    var discipline = $(this).data("discipline");
    var data = "";
    data = $('.model_' + discipline).clone().css("display", "block");

    $('#degreeModel').modal('show');
});

<script>
$(function () {
$('#formsubmit').click(function () {
var discipline = $('#value_discipline').val();
var degree = $('#value_degree').val();
var baseurl = "https://www.thecollegemonk.com";
var searchTable = ["graduate-certificate", "certificate", "post-masters-certificate", "post-graduate-certificate", "undergraduate-certificate", "post-baccalaureate-certificate", "post-doctoral-certificate"];
document.getElementById("searchForm").reset();

if (discipline != "" && degree != "") {
    if (searchTable.indexOf(degree) != -1) {
        var url = baseurl + '/best-online-' + degree + '-programs-in-' + discipline;
    } else {
        var url = baseurl + '/best-online-' + degree + '-degree-programs-in-' + discipline + '/s';
    }
    window.location = url;
}
else if (discipline != "" && degree == "") {
    var url = baseurl + '/best-online-' + discipline + '-degree-programs/s';
    window.location = url;
}
else if (discipline == "" && degree != "") {
    if (searchTable.indexOf(degree) != -1) {
        var url = baseurl + '/best-online-' + degree + '-programs';
    } else {
        var url = baseurl + '/best-online-' + degree + '-degree-programs/s';
    }
    window.location = url;
}
else {
    alert("Please select a Discipline or a Degree!")
}
});
});

<script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function(){
    var search_colleges = document.getElementById('search_colleges');
    search_colleges.addEventListener("keyup", function(event){hinter(event)});
    window.hinterXHR = new XMLHttpRequest();
});

function hinter(event) {
    var input = event.target;
    var min_characters = 0;
    if (input.value.length < min_characters ) {
        return;
    } else {
        window.hinterXHR.abort();
        window.hinterXHR.onreadystatechange = function() {
            if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
                console.log('changed');
                $("ul.search_results").html("")
                var response = JSON.parse( this.responseText );
                $("ul.search_results").innerHTML = "";
                if(response.colleges == 'No Result Found'){
                  $("ul.search_results").innerHTML = "";
                }else{
                  response.colleges.forEach(function(item) {
                    var s = '<li class="list_item"><a href=' + item.university_url + ">" + item.name + "</a>";
                    $("ul.search_results").append(s)
                  });
                }
            }
        };
        window.hinterXHR.open("GET", "https://www.thecollegemonk.com/search-colleges?term=" + input.value, true);
        window.hinterXHR.send()
    }
}
</script>

<script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function(){
    var search_city = document.getElementById('search_city');
    search_city.addEventListener("keyup", function(event){hinterCity(event)});
    window.hinterXHR = new XMLHttpRequest();
});

function hinterCity(event) {
    var input = event.target;
    var min_characters = 0;
    if (input.value.length < min_characters ) {
        return;
    } else {
        window.hinterXHR.abort();
        window.hinterXHR.onreadystatechange = function() {
            if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
                $("ul.city_results").html("")
                var response = JSON.parse( this.responseText );
                $("ul.city_results").innerHTML = "";
                if(response.cities == 'No Result Found'){
                  $("ul.city_results").innerHTML = "";
                }else{
                  response.cities.forEach(function(item) {
                    var s = '<li class="list_item"><a href=https://www.thecollegemonk.com/best-online-colleges-in-' + item.state_slug + ">" + item.city + ", " + item.state + "</a>";
                    $("ul.city_results").append(s)
                  });
                }
            }
        };
        window.hinterXHR.open("GET", "https://www.thecollegemonk.com/search-city?term=" + input.value, true);
        window.hinterXHR.send()
    }
}
</script>

<script>

function ucfirst(slug) {
    var words = slug.split('-');

    for(var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
      var word = words[i];
      words[i] = word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.slice(1);
    }

    return words.join(' ');
}

    $(".degree_results").on('click','li',function(){
      $('#search_degree').val(ucfirst($(this).data('degree')));
      $('#value_degree').val($(this).data('degree'));
      $(".degree_results").html("");
    });
     $(".discipline_results").on('click','li',function(){
        $('#search_discipline').val(ucfirst($(this).data('discipline')));
        $('#value_discipline').val($(this).data('discipline'));
        $(".discipline_results").html("");
    });
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
    function togglePanel (){
       var w = $(window).width();
       if (w <= 991) {
            $('._link_box').addClass('display_none');
            $(this).find("._link_box").addClass("footer_a_l_b");
            $(this).find("._link_box").slideToggle();
            $(this).find("._link_box").parent().find("i").toggleClass("icon-minus icon-plus");
       } else {
          $('._link_box').removeClass('display_none');
       }
    }
    $(window).resize(function(){
         togglePanel();
    });
    $(".footer-collapse").on("click", togglePanel);
</script>
